- **judgment-redteam**: 判断红队 skill。当用户做出某个决策、倾向某个方案、要下判断、说"我决定做 X"、"我觉得应该 Y"、"我想选 Z"、"我的判断是……"、"我要 xxx 了"时，必须使用本 skill。本 skill 不帮用户做判断，而是扮演最锋利的反对者，系统性攻击用户的决策，逼用户看清自己的盲点、前提漏洞、失败场景。适用于技术选型、架构决策、职业选择、投资决定、产品方向等一切需要下注的场景。不要在用户只是在征求信息（"X 是什么"）或明确请求 Claude 给建议的场景下触发。 ([zhu1090093659/growth](https://github.com/zhu1090093659/growth/tree/HEAD/platforms/gemini-cli/skills/judgment-redteam) · ★24 · 2 copies)

It does not replace the judge’s final decision; it helps convert value judgment into judicial reasoning grounded in facts, norms, and argument structure. ([THUYRan/Legal-Skills-Chinese](https://github.com/THUYRan/Legal-Skills-Chinese/tree/HEAD/English/skills/judicial-value-judgment) · ★985)
- **judicial-value-judgment**: 司法价值判断——辅助法官在权利冲突/法律不确定/比例原则场景中进行结构化价值衡量。 适用情形：识别法律价值冲突/确定裁量边界/展开价值衡量/形成裁判说理框架。 核心：六步框架（识别是否需要→锁定裁量问题→识别价值冲突→定位法律价值→确认规范基础→适用价值位阶）。 注意：本skill不替代法官最终裁判，而是将价值判断转化为有事实/规范/论证结构的推理过程。 ([vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal](https://github.com/vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal/tree/HEAD/plugins/legal-atomic/judicial-value-judgment) · ★50)
- **judicial-value-judgment**: 辅助法官在权利冲突、法律不确定、比例原则审查和裁量说理场景中，进行结构化、可审查、可论证的司法价值判断。 ([MaryHu-YR/LegalSkills](https://github.com/MaryHu-YR/LegalSkills/tree/HEAD/human_skills/skills/judicial-value-judgment) · ★1)

- **jue-literature-positioning**: Use when the contribution of a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript relative to the urban-economics frontier is fuzzy or undersold. - **jue-rebuttal**: Use when a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) decision letter (R&R or reject-and-resubmit) needs a response-letter strategy and a revision plan. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Urban-Economics-Skills/skills/jue-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jue-referee-strategy**: Use when anticipating the objections a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) referee will raise before submission, so the manuscript pre-empts them. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Urban-Economics-Skills/skills/jue-referee-strategy) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jue-replication-package**: Use when assembling the data and code deposit for a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript under its mandatory replication policy — especially geocoded, restricted, or proprietary spatial data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Urban-Economics-Skills/skills/jue-replication-package) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jue-robustness**: Use when a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript's headline spatial estimate must be shown to survive specification, spatial-scale, sorting, spillover, and inference choices before submission or in an R&R. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Urban-Economics-Skills/skills/jue-robustness) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jue-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript via Elsevier Editorial Manager — submission fee, single-anonymized review, format, the JUE: Insights short-paper track, and the cover-letter confidential-data exemption. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Urban-Economics-Skills/skills/jue-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jue-tables-figures**: Use when the exhibits of a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript must make the spatial pattern visible — maps, event-study plots, RD/boundary figures, and clean regression tables. - **jue-theory-model**: Use when a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript needs a spatial model to interpret its mechanism — a spatial-equilibrium frame, a sorting model, or a quantitative spatial model (QSM) for counterfactuals. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Urban-Economics-Skills/skills/jue-theory-model) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jue-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a question is a genuinely spatial, urban-economics contribution that fits the Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) rather than a sibling venue. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Urban-Economics-Skills/skills/jue-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jue-workflow**: Use when deciding which jue-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing manuscript work from topic through rebuttal for a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) submission.
